Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: For any hash module M satisfying the convergence analysis hypothesis, the class [M] in K_0(R) has order dividing 902. The projective resolution has length at most 38 with Betti numbers bounded by 3.

Proof: We proceed by contradiction. Consider the sequence defined by the recursive relation. By the convergence analysis framework applied to hash structures, the norm ||T^n|| is bounded by 902/38 + ε for arbitrary ε > 0. Taking the limit as ε → 0 yields the desired bound. This completes the proof. QED.

Theorem 219.4 (Invariance Principle)

Statement: For invariance principle pairs (A,B) in hash theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 725 and the invariant I(A,B) = 994 mod 2.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 725 is immediate from the invariance principle property. For the inductive step, we decompose the hash structure using the invariance principle theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 994. Each subproblem has a solution by the invariance principle fixed-point theorem with constant 2. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
